- **biostat-copilot**: 生信 / 医学研究全流程编排与审计（口径先于数值 · 一致性强于看起来对 · 混杂是主战场 · **数字一律由程序算出，不由 AI 推断**）。**何时自动加载**：用户要动手做、复核或投稿一项医学/生信分析时——如「这个方向能不能做、有没有人做过」「帮我分析这份 GWAS/组学/队列数据」「核对一下我这组结果对不对」「这个药/基因有没有因果证据」「把它写成可投稿的报告」。**覆盖七类形态**：(1) 药物靶点与因果推断——药物靶点 MR、孟德尔随机化（IVW/加权中位数/Egger/众数/RAPS/污染混合 + 异质性、留一、Steiger、Radial）、cis-eQTL/pQTL 工具变量、Wald ratio、共定位 coloc/PP.H4、药物重定位；(2) 遗传关联——GWAS 汇总统计处理与质控、λGC、独立位点与可信集、精细定位、区间基因注释；(3) 组学整合——eQTL/pQTL/TWAS/PWAS、组织与细胞类型富集、单细胞归属；(4) 临床统计——基线表、生存分析、预测模型（区分度+校准度+决策曲线）、C-index/NRI/IDI、竞争风险、混合效应；(5) 差异与通路——差异表达、共表达网络（WGCNA）、富集（ORA/GSEA/GSVA/ssGSEA）、单细胞、**双胞体检测**、批次校正 ComBat、细胞通讯配受体、免疫浸润、空间转录组、拟时序、*… ([cndoin/med-biostat-skill](https://github.com/cndoin/med-biostat-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **biostat-principles**: 研究设计、生物统计分析、科研写作、咨询交付和项目审查的共同执行原则。口径争议、结果不一致或试新方法时使用；轻量任务保持原范围。 ([KangWang42/EpiAgentKit](https://github.com/KangWang42/EpiAgentKit/tree/HEAD/skills/biostat-principles) · ★29)
- **biostat-principles**: 卫生统计/流行病学项目的底层行为原则。所有与数据分析、论文写作、咨询交付、项目审查相关的 skill，开工前必须先对齐本原则。 触发场景：(1) 任意 r-biostats / academic-publishing / consulting-delivery / epi-project-audit 任务启动前；(2) 用户说"开始分析""做这个研究""帮我写"之前；(3) 出现口径歧义、方法选择争议、结果不一致时的判定依据；(4) 用户要"试新方法/优化模型/上前沿技术"时的探索工作流（§7：backup 隔离试验→公平对照→门禁合并）。 ([Liwiec/EpiCodex](https://github.com/Liwiec/EpiCodex/tree/HEAD/skills/biostat-principles))
- **biostatistician**: Think and work like an expert Biostatistician. Use when a task calls for Biostatistician judgment. Reasons from estimands, SAPs, and error budgets; aligns ICH E9(R1), CONSORT/STROBE, multiplicity, MMRM, Cox survival, causal DAGs, and GWAS FDR while treating immortal time, ICEs, and batch confounding as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/biostatistician/skills/biostatistician) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **biostatistician**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a Biostatistician — designing a clinical trial's sample size and analysis plan, choosing a survival-analysis or censoring-aware method, preparing a DSMB safety readout, or judging whether a trial's efficacy claim survives ITT vs. per-protocol scrutiny. ([wonsukchoi/domain-experts](https://github.com/wonsukchoi/domain-experts/tree/HEAD/roles/biostatistician) · ★27)
- **biostatistician**: Expert-thinking profile for Biostatistician (clinical / computational / trial and omics biostatistics): Reasons from estimands, SAPs, and error budgets; aligns ICH E9(R1), CONSORT/STROBE, multiplicity, MMRM, Cox survival, causal DAGs, and GWAS FDR while treating immortal time, ICEs, and batch confounding as first-class failure modes. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/biostatistician) · ★13)

- **biosynthetic-diversity-global-map-visualization**: Use when you have a completed BiG-SLiCE clustering run (SQLite3 output, e.g. the pre-processed full_run_result.zip of ~1.2M microbial BGCs) and want to chart BGC class distribution and GCF novelty across GTDB taxonomy. - **biosynthetic-domain-pfam-selection**: Use when constructing or curating the domain feature space for BGC feature fingerprinting — e.g., selecting core biosynthetic Pfam pHMMs for BiG-SLiCE runs, or rebuilding/validating the sub-Pfam (sublevel) signature model library. - **biosynthetic-gene-block-extraction**: Use when the input is antiSMASH GenBank (.gbk) output for a biosynthetic gene cluster (e.g. from MIBiG) and collinear gene blocks with their module/domain composition and a valid module ordering must be extracted for downstream PKS/NRPS visualisation. - **biosynthetic-gene-cluster-annotation**: Use when when you have BGC sequences in FASTA or GenBank format and need to identify and extract biosynthetic Pfam domains for feature-based clustering, similarity search, or functional characterization. - **biosynthetic-gene-cluster-detection**: Use when mining assembled bacterial (or fungal) genome FASTA files for BGCs and needing reduced false positive rates or detection of BGC classes unseen during training, especially when ClusterFinder or antiSMASH yields too many loose predictions. - **biosynthetic-gene-cluster-detection**: Use when comparing metagenome assemblies by biologically meaningful content — counting BGCs (alongside Swiss-Prot CDSs, viral sequences, and CheckM/Prokka scaffold categories) recovered per assembler per sample. - **biosynthetic-gene-cluster-detection-and-annotation**: Use when you have assembled microbial genomes (nucleotide FASTA files) and want to identify biosynthetic potential and group related BGCs for downstream linking with metabolomic data; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/biosynthetic-gene-cluster-detection-and-annotation) · 2 copies)

- **biosynthetic-gene-cluster-euclidean-projection**: Use when you need to cluster large numbers of antiSMASH-predicted BGCs into GCFs (e.g., &gt;100,000 BGCs) and pairwise network tools like BiG-SCAPE are computationally infeasible due to O(n²) runtime. - **biosynthetic-gene-cluster-metabolite-annotation**: Use when curating or re-annotating biosynthetic gene cluster (BGC) entries that carry compound-structure or substrate SMILES strings, and you need to verify SMILES syntax correctness before release. - **biosynthetic-gene-cluster-metadata-extraction**: Use when you need to enumerate or audit evidence codes and annotations (e.g. 13 NRPS substrate-selectivity evidence codes) directly from the MIBiG JSON schema or its entry JSON files. - **biosynthetic-gene-cluster-mining-with-genomic-data**: Use when you have LC-MS/MS spectra (MGF, mzXML, mzML, or mzData format) from a bacterial or fungal strain and corresponding genomic sequence data (FASTA, antiSMASH .final.gbk, or BOA .annotated.
